    jQuery('.ltheme').click(function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();

        var width = jQuery(window).width();
        if(width > 800) {
            var $el = jQuery(this).parent();
            $el.siblings().removeClass('active');

        e.stopPropagation();
    });

        jQuery('#lthemes li').removeClass('active');
    });
});

<script type="text/javascript">
        var menuElement = jQuery("#navigationpro-top-mobile");
    var metaElementCount = 1;
    console.log('Meta container elements:');
    console.log(jQuery('.nav-container-meta > ul > li'));
    jQuery.each(jQuery('.nav-container-meta > ul > li').clone(),function(i,elem) {
        console.log('Adding element #' + metaElementCount);
        var jElem = jQuery(elem);
        jElem.addClass('level0 nav-li level-top parent nav-style-accordion nav-meta nav-meta-'+metaElementCount++);
        var toggleLabel = jElem.find('a:first');
        toggleLabel.addClass('nav-a relativ-level1 level-top nav-a-with-toggler nav-a-level0');
        toggleLabel.html('<span class="nav-span">'+toggleLabel.html()+'</span>');

        var dropdownElement = jQuery('<div class="nav-dropdown level-0" style="width:auto; padding:0; height=auto;">' +
            '<div class="nav-dropdown-top"></div>' +
            '<div class="nav-dropdown-inner level-0">' +
            '<div class="nav-column-wrapper nav1-cols">' +
            '<div class="nav-column" style="width:auto;"></div>'+
            '</div>'+
            '</div>'+
            '</div>');

        var topCount = menuElement.find(' > li').length+1;

        jQuery.each(jElem.find('ul'), function(i,elem) {
            var jUl = jQuery(elem);
            jUl.addClass('level1 nav-'+topCount+' nav-ul nav-row');
            var elementCount = 1;
            jQuery.each(jUl.find('li'), function (i, elem) {
                var jLi = jQuery(elem);
                jLi.addClass('level1 nav-li nav-'+topCount+'-'+elementCount+' nav-li-column');
                var jA = jLi.find('a');
                jA.addClass('nav-a relativ-level1 nav-a-level1');
                jA.html('<span class="nav-span">'+jA.html()+'</span>');
                elementCount++;
            });
            topCount++;
        }).appendTo(dropdownElement.find('.nav-column'));

        toggleLabel.after(dropdownElement);
        toggleLabel.after('<span class="nav-toggler nav-toggler-level0 nav-accordion-toggler">.</span>');

    }).appendTo(menuElement);

    transformMenu('navigationpro-top-mobile');
    new accordion('navigationpro-top-mobile');
</script>
